<script>
const API = 'https://atlas.6845165.xyz/admin';
const ADMIN_PASS = 'auzora2026'; // change this
let allClients = [];
let editingClientId = null;

// AUTH
function login() {
  const pass = document.getElementById('admin-pass').value;
  if (pass === ADMIN_PASS) {
    localStorage.setItem('auzora_auth', '1');
    document.getElementById('auth-screen').style.display = 'none';
    document.getElementById('app').style.display = 'block';
    loadAll();
  } else {
    document.getElementById('auth-error').textContent = 'Incorrect password.';
  }
}
function logout() {
  localStorage.removeItem('auzora_auth');
  location.reload();
}
document.getElementById('admin-pass').addEventListener('keydown', e => { if(e.key==='Enter') login(); });
if (localStorage.getItem('auzora_auth') === '1') {
  document.getElementById('auth-screen').style.display = 'none';
  document.getElementById('app').style.display = 'block';
  setTimeout(() => { loadAll(); calcCosts(); }, 0);
}

// NAVIGATION
function showPage(name) {
  document.querySelectorAll('.page').forEach(p => p.classList.remove('active'));
  document.querySelectorAll('.nav-item').forEach(n => n.classList.remove('active'));
  document.getElementById('page-' + name).classList.add('active');
  document.getElementById('nav-' + name).classList.add('active');
  if (name === 'persona') initPersonaPage();
  if (name === 'phones') loadPhones();
}

// PHONE NUMBERS
let allPhoneNumbers = []; // { phoneNumber, sid, friendlyName, assignedTo: {client_id, business_name} | null }

async function loadPhones() {
  document.getElementById('phones-table-body').innerHTML = '<div class="loading">Loading...</div>';
  try {
    const apiKey = localStorage.getItem('atlas_admin_key') || '';
    // Fetch Twilio numbers and client list in parallel
    const [numsRes, clientsRes, queueRes] = await Promise.all([
      fetch(`${API}/phone-numbers`, { headers: { 'x-api-key': apiKey } }),
      fetch(`${API}/clients`,       { headers: { 'x-api-key': apiKey } }),
      fetch(`${API}/number-queue`,  { headers: { 'x-api-key': apiKey } }),
    ]);
    const numsData    = await numsRes.json();
    const clientsData = await clientsRes.json();
    const queueData   = await queueRes.json();

    const clients = Array.isArray(clientsData) ? clientsData : (clientsData.clients || []);
    const queued  = new Set((queueData.numbers || []).map(n => n.phone_number));

    // Build a lookup: phone number → client
    const phoneToClient = {};
    clients.forEach(c => {
      if (c.twilio_number) phoneToClient[c.twilio_number] = c;
    });

    const numbers = Array.isArray(numsData) ? numsData : (numsData.numbers || []);
    allPhoneNumbers = numbers.map(n => ({
      phoneNumber:  n.phoneNumber || n.phone_number,
      sid:          n.sid || n.twilio_sid,
      friendlyName: n.friendlyName || n.friendly_name || '',
      assignedTo:   phoneToClient[n.phoneNumber || n.phone_number] || null,
      inQueue:      queued.has(n.phoneNumber || n.phone_number),
    }));

    renderPhonesTable();
  } catch(e) {
    document.getElementById('phones-table-body').innerHTML =
      '<div class="empty"><div class="empty-icon">⚠️</div><h3>Failed to load phone numbers</h3></div>';
  }
}

function renderPhonesTable() {
  const filter = document.getElementById('phone-filter')?.value || 'all';
  let rows = allPhoneNumbers;
  if (filter === 'assigned')   rows = rows.filter(n => n.assignedTo);
  if (filter === 'unassigned') rows = rows.filter(n => !n.assignedTo);

  if (!rows.length) {
    document.getElementById('phones-table-body').innerHTML =
      `<div class="empty"><div class="empty-icon">📭</div><h3>No numbers found</h3></div>`;
    return;
  }

  const tbody = rows.map(n => {
    const status = n.assignedTo
      ? `<span class="badge badge-active">Assigned</span>`
      : n.inQueue
        ? `<span class="badge badge-pending">In Queue</span>`
        : `<span class="badge badge-inactive">Unassigned</span>`;
    const client = n.assignedTo
      ? `<div class="client-name">${n.assignedTo.business_name || n.assignedTo.businessName || '—'}</div>
         <div class="client-email">${n.assignedTo.client_id || ''}</div>`
      : '—';
    return `<tr>
      <td style="font-family:monospace;font-size:13px">${n.phoneNumber}</td>
      <td>${status}</td>
      <td>${client}</td>
      <td style="color:var(--muted);font-size:12px">${n.friendlyName || '—'}</td>
      <td style="font-size:11px;color:var(--muted)">${n.sid}</td>
    </tr>`;
  }).join('');

  document.getElementById('phones-table-body').innerHTML = `
    <table><thead><tr>
      <th>Phone Number</th><th>Status</th><th>Assigned To</th><th>Friendly Name</th><th>SID</th>
    </tr></thead><tbody>${tbody}</tbody></table>`;
}

// TOAST
function toast(msg, type = 'success') {
  const t = document.getElementById('toast');
  t.textContent = (type === 'success' ? '✓ ' : '✗ ') + msg;
  t.className = 'show ' + type;
  setTimeout(() => t.className = '', 3000);
}

// API CALLS
async function loadAll() {
  try {
    const apiKey = localStorage.getItem('atlas_admin_key') || '';
    const res = await fetch(`${API}/clients`, { headers: { 'x-api-key': apiKey } });
    const data = await res.json();
    allClients = Array.isArray(data) ? data : (data.clients || []);
    renderStats();
    renderDashboardTable();
    renderClientsTable(allClients);
  } catch(e) {
    toast('Failed to load clients', 'error');
  }
}

function renderStats() {
  document.getElementById('stat-total').textContent = allClients.length;
  document.getElementById('stat-active').textContent = allClients.filter(c=>c.status==='active').length;
  document.getElementById('stat-pending').textContent = allClients.filter(c=>c.status==='pending').length;
  document.getElementById('stat-n8n').textContent = allClients.filter(c=>c.n8nConfigured).length;
}

function planBadge(plan) {
  if (!plan) return '';
  const cls = plan === 'full' ? 'badge-full' : plan === 'growth' ? 'badge-growth' : 'badge-starter';
  const label = plan === 'full' ? 'Full Suite' : plan.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + plan.slice(1);
  return `<span class="badge ${cls}">${label}</span>`;
}
function statusBadge(status) {
  const cls = {active:'badge-active',pending:'badge-pending',inactive:'badge-inactive',cancelled:'badge-cancelled'}[status] || 'badge-inactive';
  return `<span class="badge ${cls}">${status||'unknown'}</span>`;
}
function formatDate(iso) {
  if (!iso) return '—';
  return new Date(iso).toLocaleDateString('en-US', {month:'short',day:'numeric',year:'numeric'});
}

function clientRow(c) {
  const n8nDot = c.n8nConfigured
    ? '<span class="n8n-dot n8n-yes"></span>Yes'
    : '<span class="n8n-dot n8n-no"></span>No';
  return `<tr>
    <td><div class="client-name">${c.business_name||c.businessName||'—'}</div><div class="client-email">${c.email||''}</div></td>
    <td>${statusBadge(c.status)}</td>
    <td>${planBadge(c.plan)}</td>
    <td>${c.industry||'—'}</td>
    <td style="font-size:12px">${n8nDot}</td>
    <td style="color:var(--muted);font-size:12px">${formatDate(c.createdAt)}</td>
    <td><button class="action-btn" onclick="openDetail('${c.client_id}')">View →</button></td>
  </tr>`;
}

function tableWrap(rows) {
  if (!rows.length) return `<div class="empty"><div class="empty-icon">📭</div><h3>No clients yet</h3><p>Add your first client to get started.</p></div>`;
  return `<table><thead><tr>
    <th>Client</th><th>Status</th><th>Plan</th><th>Industry</th><th>n8n</th><th>Added</th><th></th>
  </tr></thead><tbody>${rows.join('')}</tbody></table>`;
}

function renderDashboardTable() {
  const rows = [...allClients].sort((a,b)=>new Date(b.createdAt)-new Date(a.createdAt)).slice(0,5).map(clientRow);
  document.getElementById('dashboard-table-body').innerHTML = tableWrap(rows);
}

function renderClientsTable(clients) {
  const rows = clients.map(clientRow);
  document.getElementById('clients-table-body').innerHTML = tableWrap(rows);
}

function filterClients() {
  const search = document.getElementById('client-search').value.toLowerCase();
  const status = document.getElementById('status-filter').value;
  const filtered = allClients.filter(c => {
    const matchSearch = !search || (c.businessName||'').toLowerCase().includes(search) || (c.email||'').toLowerCase().includes(search);
    const matchStatus = !status || c.status === status;
    return matchSearch && matchStatus;
  });
  renderClientsTable(filtered);
}

// DETAIL MODAL
async function openDetail(clientId) {
  editingClientId = clientId;
  const c = allClients.find(x => x.client_id === clientId);
  if (!c) return;

  document.getElementById('modal-title').textContent = c.business_name || c.businessName;
  document.getElementById('detail-modal').style.display = 'flex';

  document.getElementById('modal-body').innerHTML = `
    <div class="detail-grid">
      <div class="detail-item"><div class="detail-label">Status</div><div class="detail-value">${statusBadge(c.status)}</div></div>
      <div class="detail-item"><div class="detail-label">Plan</div><div class="detail-value">${planBadge(c.plan)}</div></div>
      <div class="detail-item"><div class="detail-label">Email</div><div class="detail-value" style="font-size:13px">${c.email||'—'}</div></div>
      <div class="detail-item"><div class="detail-label">Phone</div><div class="detail-value" style="font-size:13px">${c.phone||'—'}</div></div>
      <div class="detail-item"><div class="detail-label">Contact</div><div class="detail-value" style="font-size:13px">${c.contactName||c.name||'—'}</div></div>
      <div class="detail-item"><div class="detail-label">Industry</div><div class="detail-value" style="font-size:13px">${c.industry||'—'}</div></div>
      <div class="detail-item"><div class="detail-label">Added</div><div class="detail-value" style="font-size:13px">${formatDate(c.createdAt)}</div></div>
      <div class="detail-item"><div class="detail-label">n8n Configured</div><div class="detail-value" style="font-size:13px">${c.n8nConfigured ? '✅ Yes' : '❌ No'}</div></div>
      <div class="detail-item"><div class="detail-label">Twilio Number</div><div class="detail-value" style="font-size:13px">${c.twilio_number||'—'}</div></div>
    </div>
    <div style="margin-bottom:14px">
      <div style="font-size:11px;font-weight:600;color:var(--muted);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:0.8px;margin-bottom:8px">Edit</div>
      <div class="form-row">
        <div class="form-group">
          <label>Status</label>
          <select id="edit-status">
            <option value="pending" ${c.status==='pending'?'selected':''}>Pending</option>
            <option value="active" ${c.status==='active'?'selected':''}>Active</option>
            <option value="inactive" ${c.status==='inactive'?'selected':''}>Inactive</option>
            <option value="cancelled" ${c.status==='cancelled'?'selected':''}>Cancelled</option>
          </select>
        </div>
        <div class="form-group">
          <label>n8n Configured</label>
          <select id="edit-n8n">
            <option value="false" ${!c.n8nConfigured?'selected':''}>No</option>
            <option value="true" ${c.n8nConfigured?'selected':''}>Yes</option>
          </select>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="form-group">
        <label>Twilio Number</label>
        <input type="text" id="edit-twilio-number" placeholder="+1XXXXXXXXXX" value="${c.twilio_number||''}" />
      </div>
      <div class="form-group">
        <label>Notes</label>
        <textarea id="edit-notes">${c.notes||''}</textarea>
      </div>
    </div>
  `;
}

function closeModal() {
  document.getElementById('detail-modal').style.display = 'none';
  editingClientId = null;
}

async function saveClientEdit() {
  if (!editingClientId) return;
  const updates = {
    status: document.getElementById('edit-status').value,
    n8nConfigured: document.getElementById('edit-n8n').value === 'true',
    twilio_number: document.getElementById('edit-twilio-number').value.trim() || null,
    notes: document.getElementById('edit-notes').value
  };
  try {
    const res = await fetch(`${API}/clients/${editingClientId}`, {
      method: 'PATCH',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify(updates)
    });
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Update failed');
    closeModal();
    toast('Client updated');
    loadAll();
  } catch(e) {
    toast('Failed to update', 'error');
  }
}

// DELETE CLIENT
let deletingClientName = '';

function promptDeleteClient() {
  if (!editingClientId) return;
  const c = allClients.find(x => x.client_id === editingClientId);
  if (!c) return;
  deletingClientName = c.business_name || c.businessName || '';
  document.getElementById('delete-client-name').textContent = deletingClientName || 'this client';
  document.getElementById('delete-confirm-name-label').textContent = deletingClientName || 'the business name';
  document.getElementById('delete-confirm-input').value = '';
  document.getElementById('delete-error').style.display = 'none';
  const btn = document.getElementById('delete-confirm-btn');
  btn.disabled = true;
  btn.style.opacity = '0.4';
  btn.style.cursor = 'not-allowed';
  document.getElementById('delete-modal').style.display = 'flex';
}

function closeDeleteModal() {
  document.getElementById('delete-modal').style.display = 'none';
}

function checkDeleteConfirm() {
  const val = document.getElementById('delete-confirm-input').value;
  const btn = document.getElementById('delete-confirm-btn');
  const match = val === deletingClientName;
  btn.disabled = !match;
  btn.style.opacity = match ? '1' : '0.4';
  btn.style.cursor = match ? 'pointer' : 'not-allowed';
}

async function confirmDeleteClient() {
  if (!editingClientId) return;
  const btn = document.getElementById('delete-confirm-btn');
  const errEl = document.getElementById('delete-error');
  btn.disabled = true;
  btn.textContent = 'Deleting…';
  errEl.style.display = 'none';
  try {
    const apiKey = localStorage.getItem('atlas_admin_key') || '';
    const res = await fetch(`${API}/clients/${editingClientId}`, {
      method: 'DELETE',
      headers: { 'x-api-key': apiKey }
    });
    const data = await res.json();
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error(data.error || 'Delete failed');
    closeDeleteModal();
    closeModal();
    toast(`${deletingClientName} deleted (calls: ${data.deleted?.calls ?? 0}, leads: ${data.deleted?.leads ?? 0})`, 'success');
    loadAll();
  } catch(e) {
    errEl.textContent = e.message;
    errEl.style.display = 'block';
    btn.disabled = false;
    btn.textContent = 'Delete Permanently';
    btn.style.opacity = '1';
    btn.style.cursor = 'pointer';
  }
}

// CREATE CLIENT
// ─── VOICE & PERSONA ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Use the same base + API key as the rest of the admin portal so requests are authorized.
const ATLAS_API = API;
const VOICES_API = 'https://atlas.6845165.xyz/api/voices';
function adminAuthHeaders(extra = {}) {
  return Object.assign({ 'x-api-key': localStorage.getItem('atlas_admin_key') || '' }, extra);
}
let DEFAULT_VOICE_ID = 'uMM5TEnpKKgD758knVJO';

let VOICES = [];
let currentPersonaClient = null;

// Module-level state vars
let adminServices = [];
let adminSpecialties = [];
let adminAvoidTopics = [];
let adminHoursMode = 'custom';
let currentAdminVoiceId = null;
let activeAdminAudio = null;

function buildVoiceDesc(v) {
  const bits = [];
  if (v.description) bits.push(v.description);
  if (v.gender && v.gender !== 'unknown') bits.push(v.gender);
  if (v.group && v.group !== 'unknown') bits.push(v.group);
  return bits.join(' · ') || 'Approved voice';
}

async function loadVoices() {
  if (VOICES.length) return VOICES;
  const res = await fetch(VOICES_API);
  const data = await res.json();
  const all = Array.isArray(data.voices) ? data.voices : [];
  DEFAULT_VOICE_ID = data.default_voice_id || DEFAULT_VOICE_ID;
  VOICES = all
    .filter(v => v && v.active !== false)
    .map(v => ({
      id: v.id,
      label: v.label || v.voice_name || v.name || 'Voice',
      gender: v.gender || 'unknown',
      desc: buildVoiceDesc(v),
      group: v.group || (v.gender === 'female' ? 'left' : 'right'),
      isDefault: !!v.is_default || v.id === data.default_voice_id,
      hasPreview: !!v.preview_url,
      preview_url: v.preview_url,
      play_url: v.preview_url
    }));
  return VOICES;
}

// ── HOURS GRID ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
const DAYS = ['Monday','Tuesday','Wednesday','Thursday','Friday','Saturday','Sunday'];
const DEFAULT_HOURS = {
  Monday:    { open: true,  from: '09:00', to: '17:00' },
  Tuesday:   { open: true,  from: '09:00', to: '17:00' },
  Wednesday: { open: true,  from: '09:00', to: '17:00' },
  Thursday:  { open: true,  from: '09:00', to: '17:00' },
  Friday:    { open: true,  from: '09:00', to: '17:00' },
  Saturday:  { open: false, from: '10:00', to: '15:00' },
  Sunday:    { open: false, from: '10:00', to: '15:00' },
};

function parseHoursString(str) {
  const result = {};
  DAYS.forEach(d => result[d] = { ...DEFAULT_HOURS[d] });
  if (!str) return result;
  const openDays = new Set();
  str.split(', ').forEach(part => {
    const spaceIdx = part.indexOf(' ');
    if (spaceIdx === -1) return;
    const day = part.slice(0, spaceIdx);
    const times = part.slice(spaceIdx + 1);
    const [from, to] = times.split('\u2013'); // en-dash
    if (DAYS.includes(day) && from && to) {
      result[day] = { open: true, from: from.trim(), to: to.trim() };
      openDays.add(day);
    }
  });
  DAYS.forEach(d => { if (!openDays.has(d)) result[d].open = false; });
  return result;
}

function buildAdminHoursGrid(hoursStr) {
  const grid = document.getElementById('p-hours-grid');
  if (!grid) return;
  grid.innerHTML = '';
  adminHoursMode = hoursStr === '24/7' ? '247' : 'custom';
  const parsed = parseHoursString(adminHoursMode === '247' ? '' : hoursStr);
  DAYS.forEach(day => {
    const d = parsed[day];
    const row = document.createElement('div');
    row.className = 'hours-row';
    row.innerHTML = `
      <div class="hours-day">
        <input type="checkbox" id="p_day_${day}" ${d.open ? 'checked' : ''} onchange="toggleAdminDay('${day}');markDirty()">
        <label for="p_day_${day}">${day.slice(0,3)}</label>
      </div>
      <div class="hours-times" id="p_times_${day}" style="${d.open ? '' : 'opacity:0.3;pointer-events:none'}">
        <input type="time" id="p_from_${day}" value="${d.from}" oninput="markDirty()">
        <span class="hours-sep">–</span>
        <input type="time" id="p_to_${day}" value="${d.to}" oninput="markDirty()">
      </div>`;
    grid.appendChild(row);
  });
  toggleAdminHoursMode(adminHoursMode, true);
}

function toggleAdminHoursMode(mode, quiet = false) {
  adminHoursMode = mode;
  const grid = document.getElementById('p-hours-grid');
  const note = document.getElementById('p-hours-note');
  const all247 = document.getElementById('p-hours-247');
  if (all247) all247.checked = mode === '247';
  if (grid) {
    const disabled = mode === '247';
    grid.style.opacity = disabled ? '0.45' : '1';
    grid.style.pointerEvents = disabled ? 'none' : '';
  }
  if (note) note.style.display = mode === '247' ? 'block' : 'none';
  if (!quiet) markDirty();
}

function toggleAdminDay(day) {
  const open = document.getElementById('p_day_' + day).checked;
  const times = document.getElementById('p_times_' + day);
  times.style.opacity = open ? '1' : '0.3';
  times.style.pointerEvents = open ? '' : 'none';
}

function buildAdminHoursString() {
  if (adminHoursMode === '247') return '24/7';
  return DAYS
    .filter(d => document.getElementById('p_day_' + d)?.checked)
    .map(d => `${d} ${document.getElementById('p_from_'+d).value}–${document.getElementById('p_to_'+d).value}`)
    .join(', ');
}

// ── SERVICE / SPECIALTY TAGS ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
function renderAdminServiceTags() {
  const wrap = document.getElementById('p-services-tags');
  if (!wrap) return;
  wrap.querySelectorAll('.s-tag-pill').forEach(p => p.remove());
  const input = document.getElementById('p-services-input');
  adminServices.forEach((t, i) => {
    const pill = document.createElement('div');
    pill.className = 's-tag-pill';
    pill.innerHTML = `${t}<button type="button" onclick="removeAdminServiceTag(${i})">\u00d7</button>`;
    wrap.insertBefore(pill, input);
  });
}

function renderAdminSpecialtyTags() {
  const wrap = document.getElementById('p-specialty-tags');
  if (!wrap) return;
  wrap.querySelectorAll('.s-tag-pill').forEach(p => p.remove());
  const input = document.getElementById('p-specialty-input');
  adminSpecialties.forEach((t, i) => {
    const pill = document.createElement('div');
    pill.className = 's-tag-pill';
    pill.innerHTML = `${t}<button type="button" onclick="removeAdminSpecialtyTag(${i})">\u00d7</button>`;
    wrap.insertBefore(pill, input);
  });
}

function addAdminServiceTag() {
  const input = document.getElementById('p-services-input');
  const val = input.value.trim().replace(/,$/, '');
  if (!val || adminServices.includes(val)) { input.value = ''; return; }
  adminServices.push(val); renderAdminServiceTags(); markDirty(); input.value = '';
}

function addAdminSpecialtyTag() {
  const input = document.getElementById('p-specialty-input');
  const val = input.value.trim().replace(/,$/, '');
  if (!val || adminSpecialties.includes(val)) { input.value = ''; return; }
  adminSpecialties.push(val); renderAdminSpecialtyTags(); markDirty(); input.value = '';
}

function removeAdminServiceTag(i) { adminServices.splice(i, 1); renderAdminServiceTags(); markDirty(); }
function removeAdminSpecialtyTag(i) { adminSpecialties.splice(i, 1); renderAdminSpecialtyTags(); markDirty(); }

function getAdminServices() { return [...adminServices]; }
function getAdminSpecialties() { return [...adminSpecialties]; }

// ── VOICE GRID ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function normalizeAdminVoiceKey(value) {
  return String(value || '').trim().toLowerCase().replace(/\s*[-–].*$/, '');
}

function resolveAdminVoiceId(...candidates) {
  const flat = candidates.flat().filter(Boolean);
  for (const candidate of flat) {
    const exact = VOICES.find(v => v.id === candidate || v.label === candidate || v.label === String(candidate).split(/\s[-–]\s/)[0]?.trim());
    if (exact) return exact.id;
  }
  for (const candidate of flat) {
    const norm = normalizeAdminVoiceKey(candidate);
    const match = VOICES.find(v => normalizeAdminVoiceKey(v.id) === norm || normalizeAdminVoiceKey(v.label) === norm);
    if (match) return match.id;
  }
  return VOICES[0]?.id || null;
}

function renderAdminVoiceGrid(selected) {
  const container = document.getElementById('admin-voice-grid');
  if (!container) return;
  if (!VOICES.length) {
    container.innerHTML = '<div style="color:var(--muted);font-size:12px;grid-column:1/-1;">No approved voices available.</div>';
    return;
  }
  const activeVoiceId = VOICES.some(v => v.id === selected) ? selected : VOICES[0]?.id;
  currentAdminVoiceId = activeVoiceId;
  const leftVoices = VOICES.filter(v => v.gender === 'female');
  const rightVoices = VOICES.filter(v => v.gender === 'male');
  const renderColumn = (voices, tone) => `
    <div class="voice-column ${tone}">
      ${voices.map(v => `
        <div class="voice-card voice-tone-${tone} ${v.id===activeVoiceId?'selected':''}" id="avc-${v.id}" onclick="selectAdminVoice('${v.id}')" style="--voice-accent:${tone === 'left' ? 'var(--accent)' : 'var(--accent-hover)'};">
          <div class="voice-card-top">
            <div class="voice-name-wrap">
              <div class="voice-name ${tone}">${v.label}</div>
              ${v.isDefault ? '<span class="voice-badge">Default voice</span>' : ''}
            </div>
            ${v.hasPreview
              ? `<button class="play-btn ${tone}" id="apb-${v.id}" onclick="event.stopPropagation();playAdminVoice('${v.id}')">▶</button>`
              : `<span style="font-size:9px;color:var(--muted);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:4px;padding:2px 4px;white-space:nowrap;">Live only</span>`
            }
          </div>
          ${v.desc ? `<div style="font-size:10px;color:var(--muted);margin-top:2px;">${v.desc}</div>` : ''}
        </div>
      `).join('')}
    </div>`;
  container.innerHTML = `${renderColumn(leftVoices, 'left')}${renderColumn(rightVoices, 'right')}`;
}

function selectAdminVoice(id) {
  currentAdminVoiceId = id;
  VOICES.forEach(v => {
    const card = document.getElementById(`avc-${v.id}`);
    if (!card) return;
    const tone = v.gender === 'female' ? 'left' : 'right';
    card.className = `voice-card voice-tone-${tone} ${v.id===id?'selected':''}`;
    card.style.setProperty('--voice-accent', tone === 'left' ? 'var(--accent)' : 'var(--accent-hover)');
  });
  markDirty();
}

function playAdminVoice(id) {
  if (activeAdminAudio) { activeAdminAudio.pause(); activeAdminAudio.currentTime = 0; }
  VOICES.forEach(v => { const b = document.getElementById(`apb-${v.id}`); if(b) b.textContent='▶'; });
  const btn = document.getElementById(`apb-${id}`);
  const voice = VOICES.find(v => v.id === id);
  if (!voice || !voice.play_url) return;
  const audio = new Audio(voice.play_url);
  activeAdminAudio = audio;
  if (btn) btn.textContent = '⏸';
  audio.play();
  audio.onended = () => { if(btn) btn.textContent = '▶'; activeAdminAudio = null; };
  audio.onerror = () => { if(btn) btn.textContent = '▶'; toast('Could not load sample', 'error'); };
}

// ── AVOID TAGS ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function renderAdminAvoidTags(topics) {
  adminAvoidTopics = [...topics];
  document.getElementById('p-avoid-tags').innerHTML = adminAvoidTopics.map((t,i) => `
    <span class="tag">${t}<span class="tag-x" onclick="removeAdminAvoidTag(${i})">×</span></span>
  `).join('');
}
function addAdminAvoidTag() {
  const input = document.getElementById('p-avoid-input');
  const val = input.value.trim().replace(/,$/, '');
  if (!val) return;
  adminAvoidTopics.push(val);
  renderAdminAvoidTags(adminAvoidTopics);
  input.value = '';
  markDirty();
}
function removeAdminAvoidTag(i) {
  adminAvoidTopics.splice(i, 1);
  renderAdminAvoidTags(adminAvoidTopics);
  markDirty();
}

// ── FAQs ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function renderAdminFaqs(faqs) {
  const list = document.getElementById('p-faq-list');
  if (!faqs.length) { list.innerHTML = '<div style="font-size:13px;color:var(--muted);">No FAQs yet — add common questions your callers ask</div>'; return; }
  list.innerHTML = faqs.map((f,i) => `
    <div style="background:var(--surface2);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:10px;padding:12px;" id="p-faq-${i}">
      <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;margin-bottom:8px;">
        <span style="font-size:10px;font-weight:700;color:var(--muted);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.8px;">FAQ ${i+1}</span>
        <button onclick="removeAdminFaq(${i})" style="background:none;border:none;color:var(--muted);cursor:pointer;font-size:14px;">✕</button>
      </div>
      <input type="text" value="${(f.q||'').replace(/"/g,'&quot;')}" placeholder="Question" oninput="markDirty()"
        style="width:100%;background:var(--bg);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:6px;padding:8px 10px;color:var(--text);font-size:12px;outline:none;margin-bottom:8px;" />
      <textarea placeholder="Answer" rows="2" oninput="markDirty()"
        style="width:100%;background:var(--bg);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:6px;padding:8px 10px;color:var(--text);font-size:12px;outline:none;resize:vertical;">${f.a||''}</textarea>
    </div>
  `).join('');
}

function addAdminFaq() {
  const cur = getAdminFaqs();
  cur.push({ q: '', a: '' });
  renderAdminFaqs(cur);
  markDirty();
}

function removeAdminFaq(i) {
  const cur = getAdminFaqs();
  cur.splice(i, 1);
  renderAdminFaqs(cur);
  markDirty();
}

function getAdminFaqs() {
  return Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#p-faq-list [id^="p-faq-"]')).map(row => {
    const inputs = row.querySelectorAll('input, textarea');
    return { q: inputs[0]?.value || '', a: inputs[1]?.value || '' };
  }).filter(f => f.q || f.a);
}

// ── PAGE INIT / LOAD ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
async function initPersonaPage() {
  const sel = document.getElementById('persona-client-select');
  sel.innerHTML = '<option value="">Loading...</option>';
  try {
    const res = await fetch(`${ATLAS_API}/clients`, { headers: adminAuthHeaders() });
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}`);
    const data = await res.json();
    const clients = Array.isArray(data) ? data : (data.clients || []);
    if (!clients.length) {
      sel.innerHTML = '<option value="">No clients found</option>';
      return;
    }
    sel.innerHTML = clients.map(c => {
      const name = c.business_name || c.businessName || c.name || 'Client';
      return `<option value="${c.client_id}">${name} (${c.twilio_number || 'no #'})</option>`;
    }).join('');
    sel.value = clients[0].client_id;
    loadPersonaClient();
  } catch (e) {
    sel.innerHTML = '<option value="">Failed to load</option>';
    toast('Failed to load clients: ' + e.message, 'error');
  }
}

async function loadPersonaClient() {
  const id = document.getElementById('persona-client-select').value;
  if (!id) return;
  try {
    const res = await fetch(`${ATLAS_API}/clients/${id}`, { headers: adminAuthHeaders() });
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}`);
    const data = await res.json();
    // Accept either { client: {...} } or the client object directly.
    currentPersonaClient = data.client || data;
    if (!currentPersonaClient || !currentPersonaClient.client_id) {
      throw new Error('Bad response shape');
    }
    await loadVoices();
    renderPersonaForm(currentPersonaClient);
    document.getElementById('persona-form').style.display = 'block';
  } catch (e) {
    document.getElementById('persona-form').style.display = 'none';
    toast('Failed to load client: ' + e.message, 'error');
  }
}

function toggleAdminAH() {
  const on = document.getElementById('p-after-hours').checked;
  document.getElementById('p-ah-body').style.display = on ? 'block' : 'none';
  document.getElementById('p-ah-off').style.display = on ? 'none' : 'block';
}

function renderPersonaForm(c) {
  // Business info
  document.getElementById('p-business-name').value = c.business_name || '';
  document.getElementById('p-agent-name').value = c.agent_name || '';
  document.getElementById('p-booking-url').value = c.booking_url || '';
  document.getElementById('p-escalation-phone').value = c.escalation_phone || '';

  // Office hours grid
  buildAdminHoursGrid(c.office_hours || '');

  // Voice + realtime settings
  const vad = c.vad_threshold ?? 0.5;
  const sil = c.silence_duration_ms ?? 700;
  const tmp = c.temperature ?? 0.7;
  const tok = c.max_tokens ?? 0;
  document.getElementById('p-vad').value = vad;
  document.getElementById('vad-val').textContent = parseFloat(vad).toFixed(2);
  document.getElementById('p-silence').value = sil;
  document.getElementById('silence-val').textContent = sil + 'ms';
  document.getElementById('p-temp').value = tmp;
  document.getElementById('temp-val').textContent = parseFloat(tmp).toFixed(2);
  document.getElementById('p-tokens').value = tok;
  document.getElementById('tokens-val').textContent = tok == 0 ? 'Unlimited' : tok + ' tokens';

  // Tone & style
  document.getElementById('p-tone').value = c.tone || 'friendly';
  document.getElementById('p-pace').value = c.speaking_pace || 'normal';
  document.getElementById('p-lang').value = c.lang || 'en';

  // Call script
  document.getElementById('p-greeting').value = c.custom_greeting || '';
  document.getElementById('p-escalation').value = c.escalation_rules || '';
  renderAdminAvoidTags(c.topics_to_avoid || []);

  // After hours
  const ahEnabled = c.after_hours_enabled || false;
  document.getElementById('p-after-hours').checked = ahEnabled;
  document.getElementById('p-after-hours-msg').value = c.after_hours_message || '';
  toggleAdminAH();

  // Advanced
  document.getElementById('p-system-prompt').value = c.system_prompt_override || '';

  // Voice grid
  const voiceId = resolveAdminVoiceId(c.voice_id, c.voice, c.voice_name, c.voice_label, c.selected_voice, c.agent_voice) || DEFAULT_VOICE_ID;
  renderAdminVoiceGrid(voiceId);

  // Services & specialties
  adminServices = [...(c.service_areas || c.services || [])];
  renderAdminServiceTags();
  adminSpecialties = [...(c.specialties || [])];
  renderAdminSpecialtyTags();

  // FAQs
  renderAdminFaqs(c.faqs || []);

  markClean();
}

function markDirty() { document.getElementById('persona-unsaved').style.display='inline'; }
function markClean() { document.getElementById('persona-unsaved').style.display='none'; }

async function savePersona() {
  if (!currentPersonaClient) return;
  const btn = document.querySelector('#persona-form .btn-save');
  btn.textContent = 'Saving...'; btn.disabled = true;

  const voiceMeta = VOICES.find(v => v.id === currentAdminVoiceId) || VOICES[0] || null;
  const payload = {
    // Business info
    business_name:    document.getElementById('p-business-name').value,
    agent_name:       document.getElementById('p-agent-name').value,
    office_hours:     buildAdminHoursString(),
    booking_url:      document.getElementById('p-booking-url').value,
    escalation_phone: document.getElementById('p-escalation-phone').value,
    // Services & specialties
    service_areas:    getAdminServices(),
    services:         getAdminServices(),
    specialties:      getAdminSpecialties(),
    // Voice (all field aliases)
    voice_id:         voiceMeta?.id,
    voice:            voiceMeta?.id,
    voice_name:       voiceMeta?.label,
    voice_label:      voiceMeta?.label,
    selected_voice:   voiceMeta?.id,
    agent_voice:      voiceMeta?.id,
    // OpenAI Realtime (admin-only)
    vad_threshold:       parseFloat(document.getElementById('p-vad').value),
    silence_duration_ms: parseInt(document.getElementById('p-silence').value),
    temperature:         parseFloat(document.getElementById('p-temp').value),
    max_tokens:          parseInt(document.getElementById('p-tokens').value),
    // Tone & style
    tone:          document.getElementById('p-tone').value,
    speaking_pace: document.getElementById('p-pace').value,
    lang:          document.getElementById('p-lang').value,
    // Call script
    custom_greeting:  document.getElementById('p-greeting').value,
    escalation_rules: document.getElementById('p-escalation').value,
    topics_to_avoid:  adminAvoidTopics,
    // After hours
    after_hours_enabled: document.getElementById('p-after-hours').checked,
    after_hours_message: document.getElementById('p-after-hours-msg').value,
    // Advanced (admin-only)
    system_prompt_override: document.getElementById('p-system-prompt').value || null,
    // FAQs
    faqs: getAdminFaqs()
  };
  try {
    const res = await fetch(`${ATLAS_API}/clients/${currentPersonaClient.client_id}`, {
      method: 'PATCH',
      headers: adminAuthHeaders({ 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }),
      body: JSON.stringify(payload)
    });
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}`);
    const data = await res.json();
    const updated = data.client || data;
    if (updated && updated.client_id) {
      currentPersonaClient = updated;
      markClean();
      toast('Changes saved & live on next call', 'success');
    } else throw new Error('Bad response');
  } catch(e) { toast('Save failed: ' + e.message, 'error'); }
  btn.textContent = '💾 Save & Apply'; btn.disabled = false;
}

// ─── COST ESTIMATOR
function calcCosts() {
  const calls     = parseFloat(document.getElementById('c-calls').value) || 0;
  const duration  = parseFloat(document.getElementById('c-duration').value) || 0;
  const price     = parseFloat(document.getElementById('c-price').value) || 0;
  const rOpenAI   = parseFloat(document.getElementById('r-openai').value) || 0.09;
  const rTwilio   = parseFloat(document.getElementById('r-twilio').value) || 0.0085;
  const rNumber   = parseFloat(document.getElementById('r-number').value) || 1.15;

  const totalMins  = calls * duration;
  const costOpenAI = totalMins * rOpenAI;
  const costTwilio = totalMins * rTwilio + rNumber;
  const costTotal  = costOpenAI + costTwilio;
  const profit     = price - costTotal;
  const margin     = price > 0 ? (profit / price * 100) : 0;
  const costPerCall = calls > 0 ? costTotal / calls : 0;

  const fmt = n => '$' + n.toFixed(2);
  const pct = n => n.toFixed(1) + '%';

  // Cost breakdown
  document.getElementById('cost-breakdown').innerHTML = `
    <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;font-size:13px;">
      <span style="color:var(--muted);">OpenAI Realtime (${totalMins.toFixed(0)} min × ${fmt(rOpenAI)})</span>
      <span>${fmt(costOpenAI)}</span>
    </div>
    <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;font-size:13px;">
      <span style="color:var(--muted);">Twilio minutes (${totalMins.toFixed(0)} min × ${fmt(rTwilio)})</span>
      <span>${fmt(totalMins * rTwilio)}</span>
    </div>
    <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;font-size:13px;">
      <span style="color:var(--muted);">Twilio phone number</span>
      <span>${fmt(rNumber)}</span>
    </div>
    <div style="border-top:1px solid var(--border);padding-top:10px;display:flex;justify-content:space-between;font-size:14px;font-weight:600;">
      <span>Total Cost</span><span style="color:var(--red);">${fmt(costTotal)}</span>
    </div>
    <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;font-size:12px;color:var(--muted);">
      <span>Cost per call</span><span>${fmt(costPerCall)}</span>
    </div>`;

  // Margin
  const marginColor = margin >= 60 ? 'var(--green)' : margin >= 40 ? 'var(--yellow)' : 'var(--red)';
  document.getElementById('margin-analysis').innerHTML = `
    <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;font-size:13px;margin-bottom:8px;">
      <span style="color:var(--muted);">Client pays you</span><span style="color:var(--green);font-weight:600;">${fmt(price)}/mo</span>
    </div>
    <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;font-size:13px;margin-bottom:12px;">
      <span style="color:var(--muted);">Your costs</span><span style="color:var(--red);">${fmt(costTotal)}/mo</span>
    </div>
    <div style="background:var(--surface2);border-radius:8px;padding:12px;text-align:center;">
      <div style="font-size:32px;font-weight:800;color:${marginColor};">${fmt(profit)}</div>
      <div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--muted);margin-top:4px;">profit/month · ${pct(margin)} margin</div>
    </div>`;

  // Recommendation
  const breakeven = Math.ceil(costTotal * 1.5);
  const suggested = Math.ceil(costTotal * 2.5 / 10) * 10;
  let rec = '';
  if (margin < 40) {
    rec = `⚠️ Margin is thin at ${pct(margin)}. Consider raising your price to at least <strong>${fmt(suggested)}/month</strong> for a healthy 60%+ margin.`;
  } else if (margin < 60) {
    rec = `👍 Margin is decent at ${pct(margin)}. You could charge up to <strong>${fmt(suggested)}/month</strong> and still be competitive.`;
  } else {
    rec = `✅ Strong margin at ${pct(margin)}. At <strong>${fmt(price)}/month</strong> you're well-positioned. Breakeven is just <strong>${fmt(costTotal)}</strong>.`;
  }
  rec += `<br><br>Cost per call: <strong>${fmt(costPerCall)}</strong>. If a client has unusually long calls, you may want to add a per-minute overage fee above ${duration} min.`;
  document.getElementById('pricing-rec').innerHTML = rec;

  // Scale table
  const counts = [1, 3, 5, 10, 20, 50];
  const rows = counts.map(n => {
    const rev = n * price;
    const cost = n * costTotal;
    const prof = rev - cost;
    const annual = prof * 12;
    const color = prof > 0 ? 'var(--green)' : 'var(--red)';
    return `<tr>
      <td style="font-weight:600;">${n}</td>
      <td style="color:var(--green);">${fmt(rev)}</td>
      <td style="color:var(--red);">${fmt(cost)}</td>
      <td style="color:${color};font-weight:600;">${fmt(prof)}</td>
      <td style="color:${color};font-weight:600;">${fmt(annual)}</td>
    </tr>`;
  });
  document.getElementById('scale-table').innerHTML = rows.join('');
}

async function createClient() {
  const businessName = document.getElementById('new-businessName').value.trim();
  const email = document.getElementById('new-email').value.trim();
  if (!businessName || !email) { toast('Business name and email are required', 'error'); return; }

  const body = {
    businessName,
    email,
    contactName: document.getElementById('new-contactName').value.trim(),
    phone: document.getElementById('new-phone').value.trim(),
    plan: document.getElementById('new-plan').value,
    industry: document.getElementById('new-industry').value.trim(),
    notes: document.getElementById('new-notes').value.trim()
  };

  try {
    const res = await fetch(`${API}/clients`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify(body)
    });
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error();
    toast('Client created!');
    ['new-businessName','new-contactName','new-email','new-phone','new-industry','new-notes'].forEach(id => document.getElementById(id).value = '');
    document.getElementById('new-plan').value = 'starter';
    loadAll();
    showPage('clients');
  } catch(e) {
    toast('Failed to create client', 'error');
  }
}
</script>

<script>
  const PLANS = {
    beta: {
      name: 'Beta',
      price: '$199',
      priceMonthly: '$199/mo',
      tagline: 'The essential AI phone agent for your business.',
      stripeUrl: 'https://buy.stripe.com/test_bJe5kveAMev093agcM1ZS00',
      disabled: false,
      setupWindow: '48 hours',
      badgeClass: 'starter',
      cardClass: 'starter',
      priceClass: 'starter',
      btnClass: 'starter',
      baseFeatures: [
        'Default voice (Liz)',
        'Dedicated AI phone line',
        '24/7 call answering',
        'Calendar appointment booking',
        'Custom greeting & FAQs',
        'Client portal access',
      ],
      addFeatures: [],
    },
    starter: {
      name: 'Starter Plan',
      price: '$0',
      priceMonthly: '$0 for 3 months',
      tagline: '3 months free, then $199/month. No setup fee.',
      stripeUrl: 'https://buy.stripe.com/cNibIT3W81Ie2EM4u41ZS04',
      disabled: false,
      setupWindow: '48 hours',
      badgeClass: 'starter',
      cardClass: 'starter',
      priceClass: 'starter',
      btnClass: 'starter',
      baseFeatures: [
        'Default voice (Liz)',
        'Dedicated AI phone line',
        '24/7 call answering',
        'Calendar appointment booking',
        'Custom greeting & FAQs',
        'Client portal access',
      ],
      addFeatures: [],
      trialMonths: 3,
    },
    pro: {
      name: 'Pro',
      price: '$299',
      priceMonthly: '$299/mo',
      tagline: 'Everything you need to never miss a call.',
      stripeUrl: 'https://buy.stripe.com/14A7sD9gscmS0wE6Cc1ZS01',
      disabled: true,
      setupWindow: '24 hours',
      badgeClass: '',
      cardClass: 'pro',
      priceClass: '',
      btnClass: '',
      baseFeatures: [
        'Default voice (Liz)',
        'Dedicated AI phone line',
        '24/7 call answering',
        'Calendar appointment booking',
        'Custom greeting & FAQs',
        'Client portal access',
      ],
      addFeatures: [
        'Full access to all 6 voices',
        'Up to 3 dedicated AI phone lines',
        'Lead capture CRM',
        'Priority support',
      ],
    },
  };

  function renderPlan(key) {
    const plan = PLANS[key] || PLANS.pro;

    // Page header
    document.getElementById('pageTitle').textContent =
      `You\'re getting Auzora ${plan.name}`;

    // Plan card
    const card = document.getElementById('planCard');
    card.className = `plan-card ${plan.cardClass}`;

    const badge = document.getElementById('planBadge');
    badge.className = `plan-badge ${plan.badgeClass}`;
    badge.textContent = key === 'pro' ? '⚡ Pro Plan' : key === 'beta' ? '✦ Beta' : '✦ Starter Plan';

    document.getElementById('planName').textContent = plan.name;

    const priceEl = document.getElementById('planPrice');
    priceEl.className = `plan-price ${plan.priceClass}`;
    priceEl.innerHTML = `<sup>$</sup>${plan.price.replace('$','')}<sub>/month</sub>`;

    document.getElementById('planTagline').textContent = plan.tagline;

    // Features
    const list = document.getElementById('featureList');
    list.innerHTML = '';

    plan.baseFeatures.forEach(f => {
      list.innerHTML += `
        <li class="feature-item">
          <span class="feature-check">✓</span>
          <span>${f}</span>
        </li>`;
    });

    if (plan.addFeatures.length) {
      list.innerHTML += `<li><div class="feature-add-label">Pro adds</div></li>`;
      plan.addFeatures.forEach(f => {
        list.innerHTML += `
          <li class="feature-item">
            <span class="feature-check purple">✦</span>
            <span>${f}</span>
          </li>`;
      });
    }

    // Go-live step
    document.getElementById('goLiveTitle').textContent =
      `Go live within ${plan.setupWindow}`;

    // Summary
    document.getElementById('summaryPlanLabel').textContent = `Auzora ${plan.name}`;
    document.getElementById('summaryPrice').textContent = plan.priceMonthly;
    document.getElementById('summaryTotal').textContent = plan.price;
    if (plan.trialMonths) {
      document.querySelector('.summary-note').innerHTML =
        `Free for ${plan.trialMonths} months, then <span id="summaryRecurring">$199</span>/month. Cancel anytime.`;
    } else {
      document.getElementById('summaryRecurring').textContent = plan.price;
    }

    // Checkout button
    const btn = document.getElementById('checkoutBtn');
    if (plan.disabled) {
      btn.href = '#';
      btn.className = `btn-checkout ${plan.btnClass}`;
      btn.style.cssText = 'background:#334155;box-shadow:none;cursor:not-allowed;opacity:0.6;';
      btn.textContent = 'Coming Soon';
      btn.setAttribute('aria-disabled', 'true');
      btn.addEventListener('click', e => e.preventDefault());
    } else {
      btn.href = plan.stripeUrl;
      btn.className = `btn-checkout ${plan.btnClass}`;
    }
  }

  // Read plan from URL param
  const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
  const plan = (params.get('plan') || 'beta').toLowerCase();
  renderPlan(plan);
</script>

</body>
</html>

  <script>
    const WEBHOOK_URL = 'https://automation.6845165.xyz/webhook/new-lead';

    const form = document.getElementById('leadForm');
    const btn  = document.getElementById('submitBtn');

    form.addEventListener('submit', async (e) => {
      e.preventDefault();

      const name    = document.getElementById('name').value.trim();
      const email   = document.getElementById('email').value.trim();
      const message = document.getElementById('message').value.trim();

      if (!name || !email || !message) {
        // Simple shake on required fields
        [document.getElementById('name'), document.getElementById('email'), document.getElementById('message')]
          .forEach(el => { if (!el.value.trim()) el.style.borderColor = '#f87171'; });
        return;
      }

      btn.classList.add('loading');
      btn.disabled = true;

      const payload = {
        name,
        email,
        phone:    document.getElementById('phone').value.trim(),
        business: document.getElementById('business').value.trim(),
        source:   document.getElementById('source').value || 'Auzora Website',
        message,
      };

      try {
        await fetch(WEBHOOK_URL, {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify(payload),
        });

        // Always show success (webhook returns 200 instantly)
        document.getElementById('formBody').classList.add('hidden');
        document.getElementById('successState').classList.add('visible');

      } catch (err) {
        document.getElementById('formBody').classList.add('hidden');
        document.getElementById('errorState').classList.add('visible');
      }
    });

    // Clear red borders on input
    document.querySelectorAll('input, textarea').forEach(el => {
      el.addEventListener('input', () => { el.style.borderColor = ''; });
    });

    // Nav scroll behavior (same as main site)
    const nav = document.getElementById('nav');
    window.addEventListener('scroll', () => {
      nav.classList.toggle('scrolled', window.scrollY > 20);
    });
  </script>

</body>

<script>
const API = 'https://l366qoycr0.execute-api.us-east-2.amazonaws.com';
let knownIds = new Set();

function formatDT(iso) {
  try {
    const d = new Date(iso);
    if (isNaN(d)) return iso;
    return d.toLocaleString('en-US', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric', hour: 'numeric', minute: '2-digit', hour12: true });
  } catch { return iso; }
}

function formatCreated(iso) {
  try {
    const d = new Date(iso);
    const now = new Date();
    const diff = Math.floor((now - d) / 1000);
    if (diff < 60) return `${diff}s ago`;
    if (diff < 3600) return `${Math.floor(diff/60)}m ago`;
    return d.toLocaleTimeString('en-US', { hour: 'numeric', minute: '2-digit' });
  } catch { return ''; }
}

async function loadAppointments() {
  try {
    const res = await fetch(`${API}/appointments?client_id=6b6b5758-2cc5-4464-99d0-b74a7b70beaf`);
    const data = await res.json();
    const appts = (data.appointments || []).sort((a,b) => new Date(b.created_at) - new Date(a.created_at));

    const list = document.getElementById('appt-list');
    let hasNew = false;

    if (!appts.length) {
      if (!knownIds.size) {
        list.innerHTML = '<div class="appt-empty">No appointments yet — call the demo line and book one! 👆</div>';
      }
      return;
    }

    const newAppts = appts.filter(a => !knownIds.has(a.appointment_id));
    if (newAppts.length) hasNew = true;

    knownIds = new Set(appts.map(a => a.appointment_id));

    list.innerHTML = appts.slice(0, 8).map(a => `
      <div class="appt-row">
        <span class="appt-name">${a.caller_name || '—'}</span>
        <span class="appt-service">${a.service || '—'}</span>
        <span class="appt-time">${formatDT(a.datetime)}</span>
        <span class="appt-badge">✓ Confirmed</span>
      </div>
    `).join('');
  } catch(e) {
    console.error('Failed to load appointments', e);
  }
}

loadAppointments();
setInterval(loadAppointments, 4000);
</script>

</body>
</html>

    <section class="section shell" id="workflow">
      <div class="section-head">
        <div>
          <div class="section-kicker">How it works</div>
          <h2>Three steps from missed calls to booked leads.</h2>
        </div>
        <p class="section-lead">The visual direction stays restrained, but the message is concrete. Auzora is easy to understand in one glance.</p>
      </div>

      <div class="workflow">
        <article class="step">
          <div class="step-num">1</div>
          <h3>Connect your number</h3>
          <p>Point Auzora at the business line so calls go to the AI receptionist first.</p>
        </article>
        <article class="step">
          <div class="step-num">2</div>
          <h3>Set your rules</h3>
          <p>Customize the script, service area, booking logic, and what should happen when the caller needs help.</p>
        </article>
        <article class="step">
          <div class="step-num">3</div>
          <h3>Let it handle the call</h3>
          <p>Auzora answers, qualifies, and books. Your team gets the lead summary instead of a voicemail.</p>
        </article>
      </div>
    </section>

  <script>
    document.getElementById('year').textContent = new Date().getFullYear();

    const VOICE_DEMOS = {
      liz: {
        src: 'https://storage.googleapis.com/eleven-public-prod/database/workspace/af050efbdab9479ca29e5d48c00bd675/voices/uMM5TEnpKKgD758knVJO/O0FyWnfz0UI6X55jzQhm.mp3',
        text: "Hi, I'm Liz. I can answer calls, qualify leads, and book appointments.",
      },
      josh: {
        src: 'https://storage.googleapis.com/eleven-public-prod/database/workspace/110a0b43b87c4dc3a74adc986d39917c/voices/ZoiZ8fuDWInAcwPXaVeq/9EwzECjtvTDxjoH3F7fg.mp3',
        text: "Hi, I'm Josh. I sound calm, clear, and ready to help book your next appointment.",
      },
      tiffany: {
        src: 'https://storage.googleapis.com/eleven-public-prod/database/workspace/1da06ea679a54975ad96a2221fe6530d/voices/6aDn1KB0hjpdcocrUkmq/rpx5WDhu18UBjesNKE8S.mp3',
        text: "Hi, I'm Tiffany. I keep the conversation warm and make it easy to schedule a visit.",
      },
      jordan: {
        src: '/assets/audio/jordan-preview.mp3',
        text: "Hi, I'm Jordan. I stay polished, professional, and ready to help after hours too.",
      },
    };

    let currentVoiceAudio = null;
    let currentVoiceButton = null;
    const voiceObjectUrls = new Map();

    function setVoiceButtonState(btn, playing) {
      if (!btn) return;
      btn.classList.toggle('is-playing', playing);
      btn.textContent = playing ? '❚❚ Pause voice' : '▶ Play voice';
    }

    async function ensureBlobVoiceSource(name, audio, demo) {
      if (name !== 'jordan') return audio;
      const cachedUrl = voiceObjectUrls.get(name);
      if (cachedUrl) {
        if (audio.src !== cachedUrl) audio.src = cachedUrl;
        return audio;
      }
      const response = await fetch(demo.src, { cache: 'force-cache' });
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`Failed to fetch voice preview for ${name}`);
      const blob = await response.blob();
      const objectUrl = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      voiceObjectUrls.set(name, objectUrl);
      audio.src = objectUrl;
      return audio;
    }

    function stopCurrentVoice(resetButton = true) {
      if (currentVoiceAudio) {
        currentVoiceAudio.pause();
        currentVoiceAudio.currentTime = 0;
        currentVoiceAudio = null;
      }
      if (resetButton && currentVoiceButton) {
        setVoiceButtonState(currentVoiceButton, false);
      }
      currentVoiceButton = null;
    }

    function speakFallback(text) {
      if (!window.speechSynthesis) return;
      window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
      const utterance = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(text);
      utterance.rate = 1.02;
      utterance.pitch = 1;
      window.speechSynthesis.speak(utterance);
    }

    async function playVoice(name, btn) {
      const demo = VOICE_DEMOS[name];
      if (!demo) return;

      if (currentVoiceAudio && currentVoiceButton === btn && !currentVoiceAudio.paused) {
        stopCurrentVoice();
        return;
      }

      stopCurrentVoice(false);
      const audio = document.querySelector(`audio[data-voice-audio="${name}"]`) || new Audio(demo.src);
      currentVoiceAudio = audio;
      currentVoiceButton = btn;
      setVoiceButtonState(btn, true);
      audio.currentTime = 0;
      audio.onended = () => stopCurrentVoice();
      audio.onerror = () => {
        console.warn('Voice preview failed to load', name, audio.src);
        stopCurrentVoice(false);
        setVoiceButtonState(btn, false);
      };

      try {
        await ensureBlobVoiceSource(name, audio, demo);
      } catch (err) {
        console.warn('Voice preview source prep failed', name, err);
        stopCurrentVoice(false);
        setVoiceButtonState(btn, false);
        return;
      }

      const playPromise = audio.play();
      if (playPromise && typeof playPromise.catch === 'function') {
        playPromise.catch(() => {
          console.warn('Voice preview failed to play', name, audio.src);
          stopCurrentVoice(false);
          setVoiceButtonState(btn, false);
        });
      }
    }

    document.querySelectorAll('.voice-play[data-voice]').forEach(btn => {
      btn.addEventListener('click', () => playVoice(btn.dataset.voice, btn));
    });
  </script>
</body>
</html>
