<script>
const API = 'https://atlas.6845165.xyz';
let portalToken = null;
let portalClient = null;
let portalAvoidTopics = [];
let portalServices = [];
let portalSpecialties = [];

let VOICES = []; // Populated from ElevenLabs API

// Gender emoji mapping
const GENDER_EMOJI = { male: '👨', female: '👩', unknown: '🎙️' };

// Fetch ElevenLabs voices
async function loadVoices() {
  try {
    const res = await fetch(`${API}/api/voices`);
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Failed to fetch voices');
    const data = await res.json();
    VOICES = data.voices.map(v => ({
      id: v.id,
      label: v.label || v.voice_name || v.name || 'Voice',
      desc: v.description || '',
      emoji: GENDER_EMOJI[v.gender] || '🎙️',
      gender: v.gender || 'unknown',
      preview_url: v.preview_url
    }));
    return VOICES;
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('Failed to load voices:', e);
    return [];
  }
}

// ── HOURS GRID ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
const DAYS = ['Monday','Tuesday','Wednesday','Thursday','Friday','Saturday','Sunday'];
const DEFAULT_HOURS = {
  Monday:    { open: true,  from: '09:00', to: '17:00' },
  Tuesday:   { open: true,  from: '09:00', to: '17:00' },
  Wednesday: { open: true,  from: '09:00', to: '17:00' },
  Thursday:  { open: true,  from: '09:00', to: '17:00' },
  Friday:    { open: true,  from: '09:00', to: '17:00' },
  Saturday:  { open: false, from: '10:00', to: '15:00' },
  Sunday:    { open: false, from: '10:00', to: '15:00' },
};

function parseHoursString(str) {
  const result = {};
  DAYS.forEach(d => result[d] = { ...DEFAULT_HOURS[d] });
  if (!str) return result;
  const openDays = new Set();
  str.split(', ').forEach(part => {
    const spaceIdx = part.indexOf(' ');
    if (spaceIdx === -1) return;
    const day = part.slice(0, spaceIdx);
    const times = part.slice(spaceIdx + 1);
    const [from, to] = times.split('\u2013'); // en-dash
    if (DAYS.includes(day) && from && to) {
      result[day] = { open: true, from: from.trim(), to: to.trim() };
      openDays.add(day);
    }
  });
  DAYS.forEach(d => { if (!openDays.has(d)) result[d].open = false; });
  return result;
}

function buildPortalHoursGrid(hoursStr) {
  const grid = document.getElementById('s-hours-grid');
  if (!grid) return;
  grid.innerHTML = '';
  portalHoursMode = hoursStr === '24/7' ? '247' : 'custom';
  const parsed = parseHoursString(portalHoursMode === '247' ? '' : hoursStr);
  DAYS.forEach(day => {
    const d = parsed[day];
    const row = document.createElement('div');
    row.className = 'hours-row';
    row.innerHTML = `
      <div class="hours-day">
        <input type="checkbox" id="s_day_${day}" ${d.open ? 'checked' : ''} onchange="togglePortalDay('${day}');setDirty()">
        <label for="s_day_${day}">${day.slice(0,3)}</label>
      </div>
      <div class="hours-times" id="s_times_${day}" style="${d.open ? '' : 'opacity:0.3;pointer-events:none'}">
        <input type="time" id="s_from_${day}" value="${d.from}" oninput="setDirty()">
        <span class="hours-sep">–</span>
        <input type="time" id="s_to_${day}" value="${d.to}" oninput="setDirty()">
      </div>`;
    grid.appendChild(row);
  });
  togglePortalHoursMode(portalHoursMode, true);
}

function togglePortalHoursMode(mode, quiet = false) {
  portalHoursMode = mode;
  const grid = document.getElementById('s-hours-grid');
  const note = document.getElementById('s-hours-note');
  const custom = document.getElementById('s-hours-custom');
  const all247 = document.getElementById('s-hours-247');
  if (custom) custom.checked = mode === 'custom';
  if (all247) all247.checked = mode === '247';
  if (grid) {
    const disabled = mode === '247';
    grid.style.opacity = disabled ? '0.45' : '1';
    grid.style.pointerEvents = disabled ? 'none' : '';
  }
  if (note) note.style.display = mode === '247' ? 'block' : 'none';
  if (!quiet) setDirty();
}

function togglePortalDay(day) {
  const open = document.getElementById('s_day_' + day).checked;
  const times = document.getElementById('s_times_' + day);
  times.style.opacity = open ? '1' : '0.3';
  times.style.pointerEvents = open ? '' : 'none';
}

function buildPortalHoursString() {
  if (portalHoursMode === '247') return '24/7';
  return DAYS
    .filter(d => document.getElementById('s_day_' + d)?.checked)
    .map(d => `${d} ${document.getElementById('s_from_'+d).value}–${document.getElementById('s_to_'+d).value}`)
    .join(', ');
}

// ── SERVICE / SPECIALTY TAGS ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
function renderServiceTags() {
  const wrap = document.getElementById('s-services-tags');
  if (!wrap) return;
  wrap.querySelectorAll('.s-tag-pill').forEach(p => p.remove());
  const input = document.getElementById('s-services-input');
  portalServices.forEach((t, i) => {
    const pill = document.createElement('div');
    pill.className = 's-tag-pill';
    pill.innerHTML = `${t}<button type="button" onclick="removeServiceTag(${i})">\u00d7</button>`;
    wrap.insertBefore(pill, input);
  });
}

function renderSpecialtyTags() {
  const wrap = document.getElementById('s-specialty-tags');
  if (!wrap) return;
  wrap.querySelectorAll('.s-tag-pill').forEach(p => p.remove());
  const input = document.getElementById('s-specialty-input');
  portalSpecialties.forEach((t, i) => {
    const pill = document.createElement('div');
    pill.className = 's-tag-pill';
    pill.innerHTML = `${t}<button type="button" onclick="removeSpecialtyTag(${i})">\u00d7</button>`;
    wrap.insertBefore(pill, input);
  });
}

function addServiceTag() {
  const input = document.getElementById('s-services-input');
  const val = input.value.trim().replace(/,$/, '');
  if (!val || portalServices.includes(val)) { input.value = ''; return; }
  portalServices.push(val); renderServiceTags(); setDirty(); input.value = '';
}

function addSpecialtyTag() {
  const input = document.getElementById('s-specialty-input');
  const val = input.value.trim().replace(/,$/, '');
  if (!val || portalSpecialties.includes(val)) { input.value = ''; return; }
  portalSpecialties.push(val); renderSpecialtyTags(); setDirty(); input.value = '';
}

function removeServiceTag(i) { portalServices.splice(i, 1); renderServiceTags(); setDirty(); }
function removeSpecialtyTag(i) { portalSpecialties.splice(i, 1); renderSpecialtyTags(); setDirty(); }

// ── AUTH ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
async function login() {
  const email = document.getElementById('login-email').value.trim();
  const code  = document.getElementById('login-code').value.trim().toUpperCase();
  const err   = document.getElementById('login-error');
  const btn   = document.getElementById('login-btn');
  if (!email || !code) { err.textContent = 'Please enter your email and access code.'; return; }

  btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = 'Signing in...'; err.textContent = '';
  try {
    const res = await fetch(`${API}/portal/auth`, {
      method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ email, access_code: code })
    });
    const data = await res.json();
    if (!data.token) { err.textContent = data.error || 'Invalid credentials.'; return; }
    portalToken = data.token;
    portalClient = data.client;
    sessionStorage.setItem('atlas_token', portalToken);
    sessionStorage.setItem('atlas_client', JSON.stringify(portalClient));
    launchApp();
  } catch(e) { err.textContent = 'Connection error. Please try again.'; }
  finally { btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = 'Sign In →'; }
}

function logout() {
  sessionStorage.clear();
  portalToken = null; portalClient = null;
  document.getElementById('app').style.display = 'none';
  document.getElementById('login-screen').style.display = 'flex';
  document.getElementById('login-email').value = '';
  document.getElementById('login-code').value = '';
}

// ── APP INIT ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function launchApp() {
  document.getElementById('login-screen').style.display = 'none';
  document.getElementById('app').style.display = 'block';
  document.getElementById('topbar-biz').textContent = portalClient.business_name;
  document.getElementById('dash-title').textContent = `Welcome, ${portalClient.business_name}`;
  loadDashboard();
}

function showPage(name) {
  document.querySelectorAll('.page').forEach(p => p.classList.remove('active'));
  document.querySelectorAll('.nav-item').forEach(n => n.classList.remove('active'));
  document.getElementById('page-' + name).classList.add('active');
  document.getElementById('nav-' + name).classList.add('active');
  if (name === 'appointments') loadAppointments();
  if (name === 'settings') loadSettings();
  if (name === 'integrations') loadIntegrations();
}

// ── DASHBOARD ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
async function loadDashboard() {
  const res = await fetch(`${API}/portal/appointments`, { headers: { 'X-Portal-Token': portalToken } });
  const data = await res.json();
  const appts = data.appointments || [];
  const now = new Date();
  const thisMonth = appts.filter(a => {
    const d = new Date(a.created_at);
    return d.getMonth() === now.getMonth() && d.getFullYear() === now.getFullYear();
  });

  document.getElementById('stat-appts').textContent = appts.length;
  document.getElementById('stat-month').textContent = thisMonth.length;
  document.getElementById('stat-month-label').textContent = now.toLocaleString('en-US',{month:'long'}) + ' appointments';
  const phoneEl = document.getElementById('stat-phone');
  if (portalClient.twilio_number) {
    phoneEl.textContent = portalClient.twilio_number;
  } else {
    phoneEl.textContent = 'Not yet assigned';
    phoneEl.style.fontSize = '14px';
    phoneEl.style.color = 'var(--muted)';
  }

  const recent = appts.slice(0, 5);
  document.getElementById('dash-appt-count').textContent = appts.length + ' total';
  document.getElementById('dash-appt-list').innerHTML = recent.length
    ? apptTableRows(recent, true)
    : '<div class="empty">No appointments yet — your AI agent will fill this up! 📞</div>';
}

// ── APPOINTMENTS ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
async function loadAppointments() {
  const res = await fetch(`${API}/portal/appointments`, { headers: { 'X-Portal-Token': portalToken } });
  const data = await res.json();
  const appts = data.appointments || [];
  document.getElementById('appt-count-label').textContent = appts.length + ' appointments';
  document.getElementById('appt-full-list').innerHTML = appts.length
    ? apptTableRows(appts, false)
    : '<div class="empty">No appointments yet</div>';
}

function apptTableRows(appts, compact) {
  const rows = appts.map(a => `
    <tr>
      <td style="font-weight:600;">${a.caller_name || '—'}</td>
      <td style="color:var(--accent2);">${a.service || '—'}</td>
      ${compact ? '' : `<td>${a.caller_phone || '—'}</td>`}
      <td style="color:var(--muted);font-size:12px;">${formatDT(a.datetime)}</td>
      ${compact ? '' : `<td style="color:var(--muted);font-size:11px;">${a.notes || '—'}</td>`}
      <td><span class="badge-confirmed">✓ Confirmed</span></td>
    </tr>
  `).join('');
  const headers = compact
    ? '<tr><th>Name</th><th>Service</th><th>Date & Time</th><th>Status</th></tr>'
    : '<tr><th>Name</th><th>Service</th><th>Phone</th><th>Date & Time</th><th>Notes</th><th>Status</th></tr>';
  return `<table>${headers}<tbody>${rows}</tbody></table>`;
}

function formatDT(iso) {
  if (!iso) return '—';
  try { return new Date(iso).toLocaleString('en-US',{month:'short',day:'numeric',hour:'numeric',minute:'2-digit',hour12:true}); }
  catch { return iso; }
}

// ── SETTINGS ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
let settingsDirty = false;
let activeAudio = null;

async function loadSettings() {
  const c = portalClient;
  document.getElementById('s-biz-name').value = c.business_name || '';
  document.getElementById('s-agent-name').value = c.agent_name || '';
  buildPortalHoursGrid(c.office_hours || '');
  document.getElementById('s-booking-url').value = c.booking_url || '';
  document.getElementById('s-escalation-phone').value = c.escalation_phone || '';
  document.getElementById('s-greeting').value = c.custom_greeting || '';
  document.getElementById('s-escalation').value = c.escalation_rules || '';

  const ah = c.after_hours_enabled || false;
  document.getElementById('s-ah').checked = ah;
  document.getElementById('s-ah-msg').value = c.after_hours_message || '';
  togglePortalAH();

  // Load ElevenLabs voices then render grid
  await loadVoices();
  renderPortalVoiceGrid(resolvePortalVoiceId(c.voice_id, c.voice, c.voice_name, c.voice_label, c.selected_voice, c.agent_voice) || DEFAULT_VOICE_ID);
  renderPortalAvoidTags(c.topics_to_avoid || []);
  renderPortalFaqs(c.faqs || []);
  renderSchedulingParams(c.scheduling_params || []);
  portalServices = [...(c.service_areas || [])];
  renderServiceTags();
  portalSpecialties = [...(c.specialties || [])];
  renderSpecialtyTags();
  settingsDirty = false;
  document.getElementById('s-unsaved').style.display = 'none';
}

function setDirty() {
  settingsDirty = true;
  document.getElementById('s-unsaved').style.display = 'inline';
}

function togglePortalAH() {
  const on = document.getElementById('s-ah').checked;
  document.getElementById('s-ah-body').style.display = on ? 'block' : 'none';
  document.getElementById('s-ah-off').style.display = on ? 'none' : 'block';
}

// Voice grid
let currentPortalVoiceId = null;

function normalizeVoiceKey(value) {
  return String(value || '').trim().toLowerCase().replace(/\s*[-–].*$/, '');
}

function resolvePortalVoiceId(...candidates) {
  const flat = candidates.flat().filter(Boolean);
  for (const candidate of flat) {
    const exact = VOICES.find(v => v.id === candidate || v.label === candidate || v.label === String(candidate).split(/\s[-–]\s/)[0]?.trim());
    if (exact) return exact.id;
  }
  for (const candidate of flat) {
    const norm = normalizeVoiceKey(candidate);
    const match = VOICES.find(v => normalizeVoiceKey(v.id) === norm || normalizeVoiceKey(v.label) === norm || normalizeVoiceKey(v.desc) === norm);
    if (match) return match.id;
  }
  return VOICES[0]?.id || null;
}

function getSelectedPortalVoice() {
  return VOICES.find(v => v.id === currentPortalVoiceId) || VOICES.find(v => document.getElementById(`pvc-${v.id}`)?.classList.contains('selected')) || VOICES[0] || null;
}

function getPortalVoice() {
  return currentPortalVoiceId || getSelectedPortalVoice()?.id || null;
}

function renderPortalVoiceGrid(selected) {
  if (VOICES.length === 0) {
    document.getElementById('portal-voice-grid').innerHTML = '<div style="color:var(--muted);font-size:12px;grid-column:1/-1;">No approved voices available.</div>';
    return;
  }
  const activeVoiceId = VOICES.some(v => v.id === selected) ? selected : VOICES[0]?.id;
  currentPortalVoiceId = activeVoiceId;
  const leftVoices = VOICES.filter(v => v.gender === 'female');
  const rightVoices = VOICES.filter(v => v.gender === 'male');
  const renderColumn = (voices, tone) => `
    <div class="voice-column ${tone}">
      ${voices.map(v => `
        <div class="voice-card voice-tone-${tone} ${v.id===activeVoiceId?'selected':''}" id="pvc-${v.id}" onclick="selectPortalVoice('${v.id}')" style="--voice-accent:${tone === 'left' ? 'var(--accent2)' : 'var(--accent)'};">
          <div class="voice-card-top">
            <div class="voice-name-wrap">
              <div class="voice-name ${tone}">${v.label}</div>
              ${v.isDefault ? '<span class="voice-badge">Default voice</span>' : ''}
            </div>
            <button class="play-btn ${tone}" id="ppb-${v.id}" onclick="event.stopPropagation();playPortalVoice('${v.id}')">▶</button>
          </div>
          ${v.desc ? `<div style="font-size:10px;color:var(--muted);margin-top:2px;">${v.desc}</div>` : ''}
        </div>
      `).join('')}
    </div>`;
  document.getElementById('portal-voice-grid').innerHTML = `${renderColumn(leftVoices, 'left')}${renderColumn(rightVoices, 'right')}`;
}

function selectPortalVoice(id) {
  currentPortalVoiceId = id;
  VOICES.forEach(v => {
    const card = document.getElementById(`pvc-${v.id}`);
    if (!card) return;
    const tone = v.gender === 'female' ? 'left' : 'right';
    card.className = `voice-card voice-tone-${tone} ${v.id===id?'selected':''}`;
    card.style.setProperty('--voice-accent', tone === 'left' ? 'var(--accent)' : 'var(--accent2)');
  });
  setDirty();
}

function getPortalVoice() {
  return VOICES.find(v => document.getElementById(`pvc-${v.id}`)?.classList.contains('selected'))?.id || VOICES[0]?.id || null;
}

function playPortalVoice(id) {
  if (activeAudio) { activeAudio.pause(); activeAudio.currentTime = 0; }
  VOICES.forEach(v => { const b = document.getElementById(`ppb-${v.id}`); if(b) b.textContent='▶'; });
  const btn = document.getElementById(`ppb-${id}`);
  const voice = VOICES.find(v => v.id === id);
  if (!voice || !voice.preview_url) return;
  const audio = new Audio(voice.preview_url);
  activeAudio = audio;
  btn.textContent = '⏸';
  audio.play();
  audio.onended = () => { btn.textContent = '▶'; activeAudio = null; };
}

// Avoid tags
function renderPortalAvoidTags(topics) {
  portalAvoidTopics = [...topics];
  document.getElementById('s-avoid-tags').innerHTML = portalAvoidTopics.map((t,i) => `
    <span class="tag">${t}<span class="tag-x" onclick="removePortalAvoidTag(${i})">×</span></span>
  `).join('');
}
function addPortalAvoidTag() {
  const input = document.getElementById('s-avoid-input');
  const val = input.value.trim().replace(/,$/, '');
  if (!val) return;
  portalAvoidTopics.push(val);
  renderPortalAvoidTags(portalAvoidTopics);
  input.value = '';
  setDirty();
}
function removePortalAvoidTag(i) {
  portalAvoidTopics.splice(i, 1);
  renderPortalAvoidTags(portalAvoidTopics);
  setDirty();
}

// FAQs
function renderPortalFaqs(faqs) {
  const list = document.getElementById('s-faq-list');
  if (!faqs.length) { list.innerHTML = '<div style="font-size:13px;color:var(--muted);">No FAQs yet — add common questions your callers ask</div>'; return; }
  list.innerHTML = faqs.map((f,i) => `
    <div style="background:var(--surface2);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:10px;padding:12px;" id="pfaq-${i}">
      <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;margin-bottom:8px;">
        <span style="font-size:10px;font-weight:700;color:var(--muted);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.8px;">FAQ ${i+1}</span>
        <button onclick="removePortalFaq(${i})" style="background:none;border:none;color:var(--muted);cursor:pointer;font-size:14px;">✕</button>
      </div>
      <input type="text" value="${(f.q||'').replace(/"/g,'&quot;')}" placeholder="Question" oninput="setDirty()"
        style="width:100%;background:var(--bg);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:6px;padding:8px 10px;color:var(--text);font-size:12px;outline:none;margin-bottom:8px;" />
      <textarea placeholder="Answer" rows="2" oninput="setDirty()"
        style="width:100%;background:var(--bg);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:6px;padding:8px 10px;color:var(--text);font-size:12px;outline:none;resize:vertical;">${f.a||''}</textarea>
    </div>
  `).join('');
}

function addPortalFaq() {
  const cur = getPortalFaqs();
  cur.push({ q: '', a: '' });
  renderPortalFaqs(cur);
  setDirty();
}

function removePortalFaq(i) {
  const cur = getPortalFaqs();
  cur.splice(i, 1);
  renderPortalFaqs(cur);
  setDirty();
}

function getPortalFaqs() {
  return Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#s-faq-list [id^="pfaq-"]')).map(row => {
    const inputs = row.querySelectorAll('input, textarea');
    return { q: inputs[0]?.value || '', a: inputs[1]?.value || '' };
  }).filter(f => f.q || f.a);
}

// Scheduling Parameters
function renderSchedulingParams(params) {
  const list = document.getElementById('s-sched-list');
  if (!params.length) {
    list.innerHTML = '<div style="font-size:13px;color:var(--muted);">No parameters yet — add fields like Time Zone, Contact Email, or Service Type</div>';
    return;
  }
  list.innerHTML = params.map((p, i) => `
    <div style="background:var(--surface2);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:10px;padding:12px;" id="psched-${i}">
      <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;margin-bottom:8px;">
        <span style="font-size:10px;font-weight:700;color:var(--muted);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.8px;">Parameter ${i + 1}</span>
        <button onclick="removeSchedulingParam(${i})" style="background:none;border:none;color:var(--muted);cursor:pointer;font-size:14px;">✕</button>
      </div>
      <input type="text" value="${(p.label || '').replace(/"/g, '&quot;')}" placeholder="Field name (e.g. Time Zone, Contact Email)" oninput="setDirty()"
        style="width:100%;background:var(--bg);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:6px;padding:8px 10px;color:var(--text);font-size:12px;outline:none;margin-bottom:8px;" />
      <label style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;font-size:12px;color:var(--muted);cursor:pointer;">
        <input type="checkbox" ${p.required ? 'checked' : ''} onchange="setDirty()" style="accent-color:var(--accent);" />
        Required — AI must always collect this before booking
      </label>
    </div>
  `).join('');
}

function addSchedulingParam() {
  const cur = getSchedulingParams();
  cur.push({ label: '', required: true });
  renderSchedulingParams(cur);
  setDirty();
}

function removeSchedulingParam(i) {
  const cur = getSchedulingParams();
  cur.splice(i, 1);
  renderSchedulingParams(cur);
  setDirty();
}

function getSchedulingParams() {
  return Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#s-sched-list [id^="psched-"]')).map(row => {
    const input = row.querySelector('input[type="text"]');
    const checkbox = row.querySelector('input[type="checkbox"]');
    return { label: input?.value || '', required: checkbox?.checked || false };
  }).filter(p => p.label);
}

// Save
async function saveSettings() {
  const btn = document.getElementById('s-save-btn');
  btn.textContent = 'Saving...'; btn.disabled = true;

  const payload = {
    business_name:       document.getElementById('s-biz-name').value,
    agent_name:          document.getElementById('s-agent-name').value,
    office_hours:        buildPortalHoursString(),
    service_areas:       [...portalServices],
    specialties:         [...portalSpecialties],
    escalation_phone:    document.getElementById('s-escalation-phone').value,
    booking_url:         document.getElementById('s-booking-url').value,
    voice:               getPortalVoice(),
    custom_greeting:     document.getElementById('s-greeting').value,
    escalation_rules:    document.getElementById('s-escalation').value,
    topics_to_avoid:     portalAvoidTopics,
    after_hours_enabled: document.getElementById('s-ah').checked,
    after_hours_message: document.getElementById('s-ah-msg').value,
    faqs:                getPortalFaqs(),
    scheduling_params:   getSchedulingParams()
  };

  try {
    const res = await fetch(`${API}/portal/settings`, {
      method: 'PATCH',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'X-Portal-Token': portalToken },
      body: JSON.stringify(payload)
    });
    const data = await res.json();
    if (data.client) {
      portalClient = data.client;
      sessionStorage.setItem('atlas_client', JSON.stringify(portalClient));
      settingsDirty = false;
      document.getElementById('s-unsaved').style.display = 'none';
      showToast('Settings saved — live on next call ✓', 'success');
    } else throw new Error(data.error || 'Save failed');
  } catch(e) { showToast('Save failed: ' + e.message, 'error'); }

  btn.textContent = '💾 Save & Apply'; btn.disabled = false;
}

// ── TOAST ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function showToast(msg, type='success') {
  const t = document.getElementById('toast');
  t.textContent = (type==='success'?'✓ ':'✗ ') + msg;
  t.className = 'show ' + type;
  setTimeout(() => t.className = '', 3000);
}

// ── INTEGRATIONS ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function loadIntegrations() {
  // Webhook URL
  document.getElementById('i-webhook-url').value = portalClient.webhook_url || '';

  const gcalId = portalClient.google_calendar_id;
  if (gcalId) {
    document.getElementById('gcal-cta').style.display = 'none';
    document.getElementById('gcal-setup').style.display = 'none';
    document.getElementById('gcal-connected').style.display = 'block';
    document.getElementById('gcal-badge').style.display = 'block';
    document.getElementById('gcal-id-display').textContent = gcalId;
  } else {
    document.getElementById('gcal-cta').style.display = 'block';
    document.getElementById('gcal-setup').style.display = 'none';
    document.getElementById('gcal-connected').style.display = 'none';
    document.getElementById('gcal-badge').style.display = 'none';
  }

  const ocalId = portalClient.outlook_calendar_id;
  if (ocalId) {
    document.getElementById('ocal-cta').style.display = 'none';
    document.getElementById('ocal-setup').style.display = 'none';
    document.getElementById('ocal-connected').style.display = 'block';
    document.getElementById('ocal-badge').style.display = 'block';
    document.getElementById('ocal-id-display').textContent = ocalId;
  } else {
    document.getElementById('ocal-cta').style.display = 'block';
    document.getElementById('ocal-setup').style.display = 'none';
    document.getElementById('ocal-connected').style.display = 'none';
    document.getElementById('ocal-badge').style.display = 'none';
  }
}

function showGcalSetup() {
  document.getElementById('gcal-cta').style.display = 'none';
  document.getElementById('gcal-connected').style.display = 'none';
  document.getElementById('gcal-setup').style.display = 'block';
}

async function connectGoogleCalendar() {
  const calendarId = document.getElementById('gcal-id-input').value.trim();
  if (!calendarId) { showToast('Please enter your Calendar ID', 'error'); return; }

  const btn = document.getElementById('gcal-connect-btn');
  const status = document.getElementById('gcal-status');
  btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = 'Verifying...';
  status.textContent = 'Checking calendar access...';
  status.style.color = 'var(--muted)';

  try {
    const res = await fetch(`${API}/portal/settings`, {
      method: 'PATCH',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'X-Portal-Token': portalToken },
      body: JSON.stringify({ google_calendar_id: calendarId })
    });
    const data = await res.json();
    if (data.client) {
      portalClient = data.client;
      sessionStorage.setItem('atlas_client', JSON.stringify(portalClient));
      loadIntegrations();
      showToast('Google Calendar connected! Appointments will now sync automatically ✓', 'success');
    } else throw new Error(data.error || 'Failed to save');
  } catch(e) {
    status.textContent = '✗ ' + e.message;
    status.style.color = 'var(--red)';
  }
  btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = 'Connect Google Calendar';
}

async function disconnectGoogleCalendar() {
  if (!confirm('Disconnect Google Calendar? Existing appointments will remain, but new bookings will not sync.')) return;
  const res = await fetch(`${API}/portal/settings`, {
    method: 'PATCH',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'X-Portal-Token': portalToken },
    body: JSON.stringify({ google_calendar_id: null })
  });
  const data = await res.json();
  if (data.client) {
    portalClient = data.client;
    sessionStorage.setItem('atlas_client', JSON.stringify(portalClient));
    loadIntegrations();
    showToast('Google Calendar disconnected', 'success');
  }
}

async function sendCalendarTestEvent() {
  const btn = document.getElementById('gcal-test-btn');
  btn.disabled = true;
  btn.textContent = 'Sending...';
  try {
    const res = await fetch(`${API}/portal/calendar/test`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'X-Portal-Token': portalToken }
    });
    const data = await res.json();
    if (data.ok) {
      showToast('✓ Test event sent! Check your Google Calendar for "Test Appointment (Auzora)"', 'success');
    } else {
      showToast('✗ ' + (data.error || 'Failed to send test event'), 'error');
    }
  } catch(e) {
    showToast('✗ ' + e.message, 'error');
  }
  btn.disabled = false;
  btn.textContent = '🧪 Send Test Event';
}

function copyText(text) {
  navigator.clipboard.writeText(text).then(() => showToast('Copied to clipboard ✓', 'success'));
}

// ── LEAD WEBHOOK ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
async function saveWebhookUrl() {
  const url = document.getElementById('i-webhook-url').value.trim();
  const status = document.getElementById('webhook-status');
  status.textContent = 'Saving…';
  try {
    const res = await fetch(`${API}/portal/settings`, {
      method: 'PATCH',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'X-Portal-Token': portalToken },
      body: JSON.stringify({ webhook_url: url })
    });
    const data = await res.json();
    if (data.client) {
      portalClient = data.client;
      sessionStorage.setItem('atlas_client', JSON.stringify(portalClient));
      status.textContent = url ? '✓ Saved' : '✓ Cleared';
      status.style.color = 'var(--green)';
      showToast(url ? 'Webhook URL saved — leads will be delivered here ✓' : 'Webhook URL cleared', 'success');
    } else throw new Error(data.error || 'Save failed');
  } catch(e) {
    status.textContent = '✗ Failed';
    status.style.color = '#f87171';
    showToast('Save failed: ' + e.message, 'error');
  }
  setTimeout(() => { status.textContent = ''; }, 4000);
}

async function testWebhook() {
  const url = document.getElementById('i-webhook-url').value.trim();
  const status = document.getElementById('webhook-status');
  if (!url) { showToast('Enter a webhook URL first', 'error'); return; }
  status.textContent = 'Sending test…';
  try {
    const res = await fetch(`${API}/portal/webhook/test`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'X-Portal-Token': portalToken },
      body: JSON.stringify({ webhook_url: url })
    });
    const data = await res.json();
    if (data.ok) {
      status.textContent = '✓ Test sent';
      status.style.color = 'var(--green)';
      showToast('Test payload delivered ✓', 'success');
    } else throw new Error(data.error || 'Test failed');
  } catch(e) {
    status.textContent = '✗ Failed';
    status.style.color = '#f87171';
    showToast('Test failed: ' + e.message, 'error');
  }
  setTimeout(() => { status.textContent = ''; }, 4000);
}

// ── THEME TOGGLE ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function applyTheme(theme) {
  const isLight = theme === 'light';
  document.documentElement.classList.toggle('light', isLight);
  const btn = document.getElementById('theme-btn');
  if (btn) btn.textContent = isLight ? '🌑 Dark' : '☀️ Light';
  localStorage.setItem('atlas_theme', theme);
}
function toggleTheme() {
  const current = document.documentElement.classList.contains('light') ? 'light' : 'dark';
  applyTheme(current === 'light' ? 'dark' : 'light');
}
applyTheme(localStorage.getItem('atlas_theme') || 'dark');
</script>
</body>
</html>

/* =====================================================
   AUZ IT SERVICES — script.js
   Scroll animations, nav behaviour, form handling
   ===================================================== */

// ── NAV: scroll-aware background ─────────────────────
const navbar = document.getElementById('navbar');

function updateNav() {
  if (window.scrollY > 20) {
    navbar.classList.add('scrolled');
  } else {
    navbar.classList.remove('scrolled');
  }
}

window.addEventListener('scroll', updateNav, { passive: true });
updateNav();

// ── NAV: mobile toggle ────────────────────────────────
const navToggle = document.getElementById('navToggle');
const navLinks  = document.getElementById('navLinks');

navToggle.addEventListener('click', () => {
  const open = navLinks.classList.toggle('open');
  navToggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded', open);
  document.body.style.overflow = open ? 'hidden' : '';
});

// Close mobile nav on link click
navLinks.querySelectorAll('a').forEach(link => {
  link.addEventListener('click', () => {
    navLinks.classList.remove('open');
    navToggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
    document.body.style.overflow = '';
  });
});

// ── SCROLL ANIMATIONS (IntersectionObserver) ──────────
const animatedEls = document.querySelectorAll('.animate-on-scroll');

const observer = new IntersectionObserver(
  (entries) => {
    entries.forEach(entry => {
      if (entry.isIntersecting) {
        entry.target.classList.add('visible');
        // Once visible, stop observing for performance
        observer.unobserve(entry.target);
      }
    });
  },
  {
    threshold: 0.12,
    rootMargin: '0px 0px -40px 0px',
  }
);

animatedEls.forEach(el => observer.observe(el));

// ── ACTIVE NAV LINK (scroll spy) ─────────────────────
const sections = document.querySelectorAll('section[id]');
const navAnchors = document.querySelectorAll('.nav-links a');

const sectionObserver = new IntersectionObserver(
  (entries) => {
    entries.forEach(entry => {
      if (entry.isIntersecting) {
        const id = entry.target.getAttribute('id');
        navAnchors.forEach(a => {
          a.classList.toggle('active', a.getAttribute('href') === `#${id}`);
        });
      }
    });
  },
  { threshold: 0.4 }
);

sections.forEach(sec => sectionObserver.observe(sec));

// ── CONTACT FORM ──────────────────────────────────────
const form       = document.getElementById('contactForm');
const submitBtn  = document.getElementById('submitBtn');
const formSuccess = document.getElementById('formSuccess');

form.addEventListener('submit', async (e) => {
  e.preventDefault();

  // Basic validation
  const name    = form.name.value.trim();
  const email   = form.email.value.trim();
  const message = form.message.value.trim();

  if (!name || !email || !message) {
    formSuccess.style.color = '#f87171';
    formSuccess.textContent = 'Please fill in all fields.';
    return;
  }

  const emailRe = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/;
  if (!emailRe.test(email)) {
    formSuccess.style.color = '#f87171';
    formSuccess.textContent = 'Please enter a valid email address.';
    return;
  }

  // Simulate async submit
  submitBtn.disabled = true;
  submitBtn.querySelector('.btn-text').textContent = 'Sending…';

  await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 1200));

  form.reset();
  formSuccess.style.color = '#22c55e';
  formSuccess.textContent = '✓ Message sent! We\'ll be in touch shortly.';
  submitBtn.disabled = false;
  submitBtn.querySelector('.btn-text').textContent = 'Send Message';
});

// ── SMOOTH PARALLAX on hero orbs (subtle, perf-friendly)
let ticking = false;
const orbs = document.querySelectorAll('.mesh-orb');

window.addEventListener('scroll', () => {
  if (ticking) return;
  ticking = true;
  requestAnimationFrame(() => {
    const y = window.scrollY;
    orbs.forEach((orb, i) => {
      const speed = 0.04 + i * 0.015;
      orb.style.transform = `translateY(${y * speed}px)`;
    });
    ticking = false;
  });
}, { passive: true });
